```
你是 Terraria 复刻项目的金标维护工程师。任务:把 JS 侧已落地的行为修复镜像进 C# oracle,再生成金标基线,跑金标测试。

背景:共享单 RNG 流的位级金标体系——`tools/golden/terrain-oracle.cs`(Reset+Terrain 段)、`tools/golden/caves-oracle.cs`(terrain→beaches 链)。JS 侧(worldgen src)刚批量修复了多处 RNG/行为偏差,**oracle 必须同步修改**,否则金标测试假红/假绿。golden JSON 由 `dotnet run tools/golden/<x>.cs <seed> <evil> <out.json>` 再生。

## 已在 JS 侧落地、需要镜像进 oracle 的修复

(Reset 尾部 3 掷已由我同步进两个 oracle,勿重复加)

A. **caves-oracle.cs**(链内 pass):
1. Jungle 隧道分支方向:原版 `num4=-2; if(Next(2)==0) num4=2`(JunglePass.cs:178-182)。oracle 若写成 `Next(2)==0 → -2` 则改反。
2. ShroomPatch 游走半径:`num = Next(80,100) * (w/4200)`(cs:80569-80574,FTW ×1.5 不加)。oracle 若 num 未乘缩放则补。
3. Corruption 中心采样:`Next(500, w-500)`(cs:13976/14012),不是 380。
4. Corruption placeAltars:两点掷后 `while (oceanDepths(x,y))` 重掷(cs:14252-14256);oceanDepths(cs:10978):`y>oceanLevel→false; x<beachDistance||x>w-beachDistance→true`。oceanLevel=(worldSurface+rockLayer)/2+40(cs:4393)。
5. Lakes 湖腔主体只清 active 保留 type(幽灵格,cs:80469 等段)。
6. Tunnels 锚点沙判定 `type==53`(oracle 用原版 id 53=沙,应已正确,核对即可);DirtWallBackgrounds 雪判定 `t==147`(同,核对)。
B. 每镜像一处,先读 oracle 对应段确认它确实有该偏差(可能与 JS 不同,以原版为准),没有则跳过并记录。

## 执行步骤

1. 逐项镜像(小心:`dotnet run` 会把 .cs 当脚本跑,该环境 dotnet 10 支持文件脚本?若 `dotnet run tools/golden/caves-oracle.cs` 不支持,查该仓库历史用法:可能需要 `dotnet run --project` 或 `dotnet script`;docs/worldgen/vanilla-pass-alignment.md「种子等价工作流」节有标准命令,照它执行)。
2. 再生金标:`dotnet ...` 生成 `tests/golden/caves-chain-9293480.json` 与 `caves-chain-SandboxWorld.json`、`terrain-9293480.json`/`terrain-SandboxWorld.json`(种子与 evil 参数照 tests 里现有 golden JSON 的 header/文件名约定,先读一个现有 JSON 看格式)。
3. `npx vitest run tests/terrain-oracle.test.ts` 必须双种子全绿。
4. `npx vitest run tests/caves-checkpoint.test.ts`:注意该测试被并发会话扩展了 corruption 检查点且其 golden 可能不含 corruption 段——若失败点是「golden 缺 corruption 段」或 corruption 段哈希不等,如实报告**分歧 pass 名与两侧哈希**,不要试图改 JS 的 CorruptionPass(另一会话在调);其他段必须全绿。
5. 报告:镜像了哪些/跳过哪些(原因)/再生命令/测试结果(逐段)。

约束:只改 tools/golden/*.cs 与 tests/golden/*.json;不改 src/;不 commit。若 dotnet 环境跑不起来,如实报告卡点。
```

153	num4w *= (double)(int)r.Next(90, 110) * 0.01;
154	double num5w = num3w, num6w = num3w, num7w = num4w, num8w = num4w;
155	double num9t = (double)H * 0.23;
156	double num10 = 0.17 + 0.02, num11 = 0.26;
157	var history = new List<double>();
158	var surf = new int[W];
159	Action<int, double> Retarget = null!;
160	Retarget = (targetX, targetHeight) => {
161	    int len = history.Count;
162	    for (int i1 = 0; i1 < len / 2; i1++)
163	    {
164	        if (history[len - 1] <= targetHeight) break;
165	        for (int j = 0; j < len - i1 * 2; j++)
166	        {
167	            double v = history[len - j - 1] - 1.0;
168	            history[len - j - 1] = v;
169	            if (v <= targetHeight) break;
170	        }
171	    }
172	    for (int k = 0; k < len; k++)
173	    {
174	        int x = targetX - k;
175	        if (x >= 0 && x < W)
176	        {
177	            int ws = (int)history[len - k - 1];
178	            for (int y = 0; y < ws; y++) act[y * W + x] = 0;
179	            for (int y = ws; y < H; y++) { act[y * W + x] = 1; if (typ[y * W + x] != 1) typ[y * W + x] = 0; }
180	        }
181	    }
182	};
183	Func<int, double> GenOffset = f => {
184	    double n = 0.0;
185	    switch (f)
186	    {
187	        case 0: while ((int)r.Next(0, 7) == 0) n += (int)r.Next(-1, 2); break;
188	        case 1: while ((int)r.Next(0, 4) == 0) n -= 1.0; while ((int)r.Next(0, 10) == 0) n += 1.0; break;
189	        case 2: while ((int)r.Next(0, 4) == 0) n += 1.0; while ((int)r.Next(0, 10) == 0) n -= 1.0; break;
190	        case 3: while ((int)r.Next(0, 2) == 0) n -= 1.0; while ((int)r.Next(0, 6) == 0) n += 1.0; break;
191	        case 4: while ((int)r.Next(0, 2) == 0) n += 1.0; while ((int)r.Next(0, 5) == 0) n -= 1.0; break;
192	    }
193	    return n;
194	};
195	for (int i = 0; i < W; i++)
196	{
197	    num5w = Math.Min(num3w, num5w);
198	    num6w = Math.Max(num3w, num6w);
199	    num7w = Math.Min(num4w, num7w);
200	    num8w = Math.Max(num4w, num8w);
201	    if (counter <= 0)
202	    {
203	        feature = (int)r.Next(0, 5);
204	        counter = (int)r.Next(5, 40);
205	        if (feature == 0) counter *= (int)((double)(int)r.Next(5, 30) * 0.2);
206	    }
207	    counter--;
208	    if ((double)i > W * 0.45 && (double)i < W * 0.55 && (feature == 3 || feature == 4)) feature = (int)r.Next(3);
209	    if ((double)i > W * 0.48 && (double)i < W * 0.52) feature = 0;
210	    num3w += GenOffset(feature);
211	    if (i < leftBeachEnd + flatBeachPadding() || i > rightBeachStart - flatBeachPadding())
212	        num3w = Math.Clamp(num3w, H * num10, num9t);
213	    else if (num3w < H * num10) { num3w = H * num10; counter = 0; }
214	    else if (num3w > H * num11) { num3w = H * num11; counter = 0; }
215	    while ((int)r.Next(0, 3) == 0) num4w += (int)r.Next(-2, 3);
216	    if (num4w < num3w + H * 0.06) num4w += 1.0;
217	    if (num4w > num3w + H * 0.35) num4w -= 1.0;
218	    history.Add(num3w);
219	    if (history.Count > 500) history.RemoveAt(0);
220	    // FillColumn：y < (int)num3 空气；[.., num4) 泥(0)；≥num4 石(1)。无墙（DirtWallBg 稍后）
221	    int st = (int)num3w;
222	    for (int y = 0; y < st; y++) { act[y * W + i] = 0; }
223	    for (int y = st; y < H; y++) { act[y * W + i] = 1; typ[y * W + i] = (ushort)((double)y < num4w ? 0 : 1); }
224	    surf[i] = st;
225	    if (i == rightBeachStart - flatBeachPadding())
226	    {
227	        if (num3w > num9t) Retarget(i, num9t);
228	        feature = 0; counter = W - i;
229	    }
230	}
231	int worldSurface = (int)(num6w + 25.0);
232	double rockLayerD = num8w;
233	double num12 = (int)((rockLayerD - worldSurface) / 6.0) * 6;
234	int rockLayer = (int)(worldSurface + num12);
235	int waterLine = (int)((rockLayer + (double)H) / 2) + (int)r.Next(-100, 20);
236	int lavaLine = waterLine + (int)r.Next(50, 80);
237	int num14 = 20;
238	if (num7w < num6w + num14)
239	{
240	    double mid = (num7w + num6w) / 2.0;
241	    double spread = Math.Abs(num7w - num6w); if (spread < num14) spread = num14;
242	    num7w = mid + spread / 2.0; num6w = mid - spread / 2.0;
243	}
244	// GenVars 口径（后续 pass 消费）
245	double gvWorldSurfaceLow = num5w, gvWorldSurfaceHigh = num6w;
246	double gvRockLayerLow = num7w, gvRockLayerHigh = num8w;
247	double gvWorldSurface = num3w, gvRockLayer = num4w;
248	Checkpoint("terrain");
249	
250	Console.WriteLine($"terrain ok: worldSurface={worldSurface} rockLayer={rockLayer} waterLine={waterLine} lavaLine={lavaLine}");

252	// ================= 原语 =================
253	bool mudWallFlag = false;
254	void TileRunner(double i, double j, double strength, int steps, int type,
255	    bool addTile = false, double speedX = 0.0, double speedY = 0.0, bool noYChange = false,
256	    bool overRide = true, int ignoreTileType = -1)
257	{
258	    double num1 = strength, num2 = steps;
259	    double px = i, py = j;
260	    double vx = (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.1, vy = (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.1;
261	    if (speedX != 0.0 || speedY != 0.0) { vx = speedX; vy = speedY; }
262	    int num3l = 0;
263	    r.Next(4); // num3 液体类型（普通世界结果弃用）
264	    while (num1 > 0.0 && num2 > 0.0)
265	    {
266	        if (py < 0.0 && num2 > 0.0 && type == 59) num2 = 0.0;
267	        num1 = strength * (num2 / steps);
268	        num2 -= 1.0;
269	        int x0 = (int)(px - num1 * 0.5), x1 = (int)(px + num1 * 0.5);
270	        int y0 = (int)(py - num1 * 0.5), y1 = (int)(py + num1 * 0.5);
271	        if (x0 < 1) x0 = 1; if (x1 > W - 1) x1 = W - 1;
272	        if (y0 < 1) y0 = 1; if (y1 > H - 1) y1 = H - 1;
273	        for (int x = x0; x < x1; x++)
274	        {
275	            if (x < BEACH_DISTANCE + 50 || x >= W - BEACH_DISTANCE - 50) num3l = 0;
276	            for (int y = y0; y < y1; y++)
277	            {
278	                int ti = y * W + x;
279	                if (act[ti] != 0 && FRAMED_NOT_CUT.Contains(typ[ti])) continue;      // cs:77217
280	                if (ignoreTileType >= 0 && act[ti] != 0 && typ[ti] == ignoreTileType) continue;  // cs:77219
281	                double dist = Math.Abs(x - px) + Math.Abs(y - py);
282	                if (dist >= strength * 0.5 * (1.0 + (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.015)) continue;
283	                if (mudWallFlag && y > worldSurface && wal[(y - 1) * W + x] != 2
284	                    && y < H - 210 - (int)r.Next(3)
285	                    && dist < strength * 0.45 * (1.0 + (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.01))
286	                {
287	                    if (y > lavaLine - (int)r.Next(0, 4) - 50)
288	                    {
289	                        if (wal[(y-1) * W + x] != 64 && wal[(y+1) * W + x] != 64 && wal[y * W + x - 1] != 64 && wal[y * W + x + 1] != 64) wal[ti] = 15;
290	                    }
291	                    else if (wal[(y-1) * W + x] != 15 && wal[(y+1) * W + x] != 15 && wal[y * W + x - 1] != 15 && wal[y * W + x + 1] != 15)
292	                    {
293	                        wal[ti] = 64;
294	                    }
295	                }
296	                if (type < 0)
297	                {
298	                    if (act[ti] == 1 && typ[ti] == 53) continue;
299	                    if (type == -2 && act[ti] == 1 && (y < waterLine || y > lavaLine))
300	                    {
301	                        liq[ti] = 255; liqT[ti] = (byte)num3l;
302	                        if (y > lavaLine) liqT[ti] = 1; // lava（普通世界 remix 除外）
303	                    }
304	                    act[ti] = 0;
305	                }
306	                else
307	                {
308	                    bool flag4 = false;
309	                    ushort cur = typ[ti];
310	                    if (overRide && act[ti] == 1)
311	                    {
312	                        // Main.tileStone[type]（Main.cs tileStone[63-68,130,131,566]=true）：
313	                        // 宝石/活失活石/琥珀石铺设时仅替换石头，其余活性块保护。
314	                        // 丛林链 r1/r2/r3 与收尾 loop 都铺宝石——缺失会让宝石覆盖活性泥，
315	                        // 污染后续 type==59 拒绝采样 → 石囊位置整体错位
316	                        if ((type >= 63 && type <= 68) || type == 130 || type == 131 || type == 566)
317	                        {
318	                            if (cur != 1) flag4 = true;
319	                        }
320	                        if (NOT_CLEAR.Contains(cur)) flag4 = true;
321	                        if (cur == 1 && type == 59)
322	                        {
323	                            int f4r = (int)r.Next(-50, 50);
324	                            if (y < worldSurface + f4r) flag4 = true;
325	                        }
326	                        else if (FLAG3_ALWAYS.Contains(cur)) flag4 = true;
327	                        else if (cur == 53)
328	                        {
329	                            if (type == 40) flag4 = true;
330	                            if (y < worldSurface && type != 59) flag4 = true;
331	                        }
332	                        else if (cur == 396 || cur == 397) flag4 = !IsOre(type);
333	                        else if (cur == 367 || cur == 368) { if (type == 59) flag4 = true; }
334	                    }
335	                    if (!flag4) typ[ti] = (ushort)type;
336	                    if (addTile) { act[ti] = 1; liq[ti] = 0; }
337	                    if (noYChange && y < worldSurface && type != 59) wal[ti] = 2;
338	                    if (type == 59 && y > waterLine && liq[ti] > 0) liq[ti] = 0;
339	                }
340	            }
341	        }
342	        px += vx; py += vy;
343	        // 大半径阶梯（cs:77370-77450）：50..900 每档 val+=vel, num2-=1, 2 发速度骰（Y 先 X 后）
344	        double[] thrs = { 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300, 400, 500, 600, 700, 800, 900 };
345	        foreach (var thr in thrs)
346	        {
347	            if (num1 > thr)
348	            {
349	                px += vx; py += vy; num2 -= 1.0;
350	                vy += (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.05;
351	                vx += (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.05;
352	            }
353	            else break;
354	        }
355	        vx += (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.05;
356	        if (vx > 1.0) vx = 1.0; if (vx < -1.0) vx = -1.0;
357	        if (!noYChange)
358	        {
359	            vy += (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.05;
360	            if (vy > 1.0) vy = 1.0; if (vy < -1.0) vy = -1.0;
361	        }
362	        else if (type != 59 && num1 < 3.0)
363	        {
364	            if (vy > 1.0) vy = 1.0; if (vy < -1.0) vy = -1.0;
365	        }
366	        if (type == 59 && !noYChange)
367	        {
368	            if (vy > 0.5) vy = 0.5; if (vy < -0.5) vy = -0.5;
369	            if (py < rockLayer + 100.0) vy = 1.0;
370	            if (py > H - 300) vy = -1.0;
371	        }
372	    }
373	}
374	// TileID.Sets.Ore（TileID.cs:399）：19 个。曾误抄成 20/203/70/21/116/234/1110 的杂表
375	// → 沙岩(396/397)上铺矿的 flag4 判定与 JS ORE_SET 分叉（ores checkpoint 纯 type 差）
376	static bool IsOre(int t) =>
377	    t is 7 or 166 or 6 or 167 or 9 or 168 or 8 or 169 or 22 or 204 or 37 or 58
378	      or 107 or 221 or 108 or 222 or 111 or 223 or 211;
379	
380	(double, double) DigTunnel(double X, double Y, double xDir, double yDir, int steps, int size, bool wet = false)
381	{
382	    double x = X, y = Y;
383	    x = Math.Clamp(x, size + 1.0, W - size - 1.0);
384	    y = Math.Clamp(y, size + 1.0, H - size - 1.0);
385	    double n3 = 0.0, n4 = 0.0, radius = size;
386	    for (int s = 0; s < steps; s++)
387	    {
388	        int x0 = (int)(x - radius), x1 = (int)(x + radius);
389	        int y0 = (int)(y - radius), y1 = (int)(y + radius);
390	        for (int ix = x0; ix <= x1; ix++)
391	        {
392	            for (int iy = y0; iy <= y1; iy++)
393	            {
394	                if (Math.Abs(ix - x) + Math.Abs(iy - y) < radius * (1.0 + (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.005)
395	                    && ix >= 0 && ix < W && iy >= 0 && iy < H)
396	                {
397	                    int ti = iy * W + ix;
398	                    act[ti] = 0;
399	                    if (wet) { liq[ti] = 255; liqT[ti] = 0; }
400	                }
401	            }
402	        }
403	        radius += (int)r.Next(-50, 51) * 0.03;
404	        if (radius < size * 0.6) radius = size * 0.6;
405	        if (radius > size * 2) radius = size * 2;
406	        n3 += (int)r.Next(-20, 21) * 0.01;
407	        n4 += (int)r.Next(-20, 21) * 0.01;
408	        if (n3 < -1.0) n3 = -1.0; if (n3 > 1.0) n3 = 1.0;
409	        if (n4 < -1.0) n4 = -1.0; if (n4 > 1.0) n4 = 1.0;
410	        x += (xDir + n3) * 0.6;
411	        y += (yDir + n4) * 0.6;
412	    }
413	    return (x, y);
414	}
415	
416	void Caverer(int X, int Y)
417	{
418	    if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0)
419	    {
420	        int segs = (int)r.Next(7, 9);
421	        (double dx, double dy) = RandDir();
422	        double px = X, py = Y;
423	        for (int k = 0; k < segs; k++)
424	        {
425	            (px, py) = DigTunnel(px, py, dx, dy, (int)r.Next(6, 20), (int)r.Next(4, 9));
426	            dx += (int)r.Next(-20, 21) * 0.1; dy += (int)r.Next(-20, 21) * 0.1;
427	            if (dx < -1.5) dx = -1.5; if (dx > 1.5) dx = 1.5;
428	            if (dy < -1.5) dy = -1.5; if (dy > 1.5) dy = 1.5;
429	            var (bx, by) = RandDir();
430	            var (ex, ey) = DigTunnel(px, py, bx, by, (int)r.Next(30, 50), (int)r.Next(3, 6));
431	            TileRunner((int)ex, (int)ey, (int)r.Next(10, 20), (int)r.Next(5, 10), -1);
432	        }
433	    }
434	    else
435	    {
436	        int segs = (int)r.Next(15, 30);
437	        (double dx, double dy) = RandDir();
438	        double px = X, py = Y;
439	        for (int k = 0; k < segs; k++)
440	        {
441	            (px, py) = DigTunnel(px, py, dx, dy, (int)r.Next(5, 15), (int)r.Next(2, 6), true);
442	            dx += (int)r.Next(-20, 21) * 0.1; dy += (int)r.Next(-20, 21) * 0.1;
443	            if (dx < -1.5) dx = -1.5; if (dx > 1.5) dx = 1.5;
444	            if (dy < -1.5) dy = -1.5; if (dy > 1.5) dy = 1.5;
445	        }
446	    }
447	}
448	(double, double) RandDir()
449	{
450	    double dx = (int)r.Next(0, 100) * 0.01, dy = 1.0 - dx;
451	    if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0) dx = -dx;
452	    if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0) dy = -dy;
453	    return (dx, dy);
454	}

456	int mountRolls = 0;
457	void Mountinater(int i, int j)
458	{
459	    double num = (int)r.Next(80, 120);
460	    double num2 = num;
461	    double num3 = (int)r.Next(40, 55);
462	    double px = i, py = j + num3 / 2.0;
463	    double vx = (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.1, vy = (int)r.Next(-20, -10) * 0.1;
464	    while (num > 0.0 && num3 > 0.0)
465	    {
466	        num -= (int)r.Next(4);
467	        num3 -= 1.0;
468	        int x0 = (int)(px - num * 0.5), x1 = (int)(px + num * 0.5);
469	        int y0 = (int)(py - num * 0.5), y1 = (int)(py + num * 0.5);
470	        if (x0 < 0) x0 = 0; if (x1 > W) x1 = W;
471	        if (y0 < 0) y0 = 0; if (y1 > H) y1 = H;
472	        num2 = num * (int)r.Next(80, 120) * 0.01;
473	        for (int x = x0; x < x1; x++)
474	            for (int y = y0; y < y1; y++)
475	            {
476	                double ddx = Math.Abs(x - px), ddy = Math.Abs(y - py);
477	                if (Math.Sqrt(ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy) < num2 * 0.4 && act[y * W + x] == 0)
478	                { act[y * W + x] = 1; typ[y * W + x] = 0; }
479	            }
480	        px += vx; py += vy;
481	        vx += (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.05;
482	        vy += (int)r.Next(-10, 11) * 0.05;
483	        if (vx > 0.5) vx = 0.5; if (vx < -0.5) vx = -0.5;
484	        if (vy > -0.5) vy = -0.5; if (vy < -1.5) vy = -1.5;
485	    }
486	}

488	// ================= Pass 1：Dunes（cs:11540-11602 + DunesBiome.cs + SurfaceMap.cs）=================
489	r.Next(3); // DungeonCrawler.SetupDungeonGenVarVariables（cs:11542 + DungeonCrawler.cs:56）
490	// Count：WorldGenRange(1,2,ScaleWith:WorldWidth) → Next((int)(w/4200*1), (int)(w/4200*2)+1)
491	int dunesCount = (int)r.Next((int)(W / 4200.0 * 1), (int)(W / 4200.0 * 2) + 1);
492	var pyrX = new List<int>(); var pyrY = new List<int>();
493	for (int i = 0; i < dunesCount; i++)
494	{
495	    int ox, oy, tries = 0;
496	    for (; ; )
497	    {
498	        ox = (int)r.Next(500, W - 500);
499	        oy = (int)r.Next(0, H);
500	        bool fJungle = Math.Abs(ox - jungleX) < (int)(600.0 * (W / 4200.0));
501	        bool fCenter = Math.Abs(ox - W / 2) < 300;
502	        bool fSnow = ox > snowL - 300 && ox < snowR + 300;
503	        tries++;
504	        if (tries >= W) fJungle = false;
505	        if (tries >= W * 2) fSnow = false;
506	        if (!(fJungle || fCenter || fSnow)) break;
507	    }
508	    DunesPlace(ox, oy);
509	    if ((double)r.NextDouble() <= 0.8)
510	    {
511	        int px2 = (int)r.Next(ox - 200, ox + 200);
512	        for (int j = 0; j < H; j++)
513	        {
514	            if (act[j * W + px2] == 1) { pyrX.Add(px2); pyrY.Add(j + 20); break; }
515	        }
516	    }
517	}
518	void DunesPlace(int originX, int originY)
519	{
520	    // DunesBiome.Place（DunesBiome.cs:56-70）：heightScale=1（Configuration.json）
521	    int height = (int)((int)r.Next(60, 100) * 1.0);
522	    int height2 = (int)((int)r.Next(60, 100) * 1.0);
523	    // SingleDunesWidth：Min150 Max250 ScaleWith=None → Next(150,251) 不缩放
524	    int rand1 = (int)r.Next(150, 251);
525	    int rand2 = (int)r.Next(150, 251);
526	    // ★CreateFromPlacement ×2（SurfaceMap 采样+风向掷）都先于 PlaceSingle ×2
527	    //（DunesBiome.cs:62-65：两图均为 pre-dune1 地形）
528	    int l1 = originX - rand1 / 2 + 30 - rand1 / 2, l2 = originX + rand2 / 2 - 30 - rand2 / 2;
529	    var surf1 = SurfaceFromArea(l1 - 20, rand1 + 40);
530	    bool wind1 = (int)r.Next(2) != 0;
531	    var surf2 = SurfaceFromArea(l2 - 20, rand2 + 40);
532	    bool wind2 = (int)r.Next(2) != 0;
533	    DunesSingle(l1, originY, rand1, height, wind1, surf1);
534	    DunesSingle(l2, originY, rand2, height2, wind2, surf2);
535	}
536	void DunesSingle(int left, int originY, int width, int height, bool windRight, int[] surface)
537	{
538	    int top = originY - height / 2;
539	    int centerX = left + width / 2;
540	    int num = (int)r.Next(3) + 8;
541	    for (int i = 0; i < num - 1; i++)
542	    {
543	        int num2 = (int)(2.0 / num * width);
544	        int num3 = (int)((double)i / num * width + left) + num2 * 2 / 5;
545	        num3 += (int)r.Next(-5, 6);
546	        double num4 = (double)i / (num - 2);
547	        double num5 = 1.0 - Math.Abs(num4 - 0.5) * 2.0;
548	        PlaceHill(num3 - num2 / 2, num3 + num2 / 2, num5 * 0.3 + 0.2, surface, left, width, centerX, windRight);
549	    }
550	    int num6 = (int)r.Next(2) + 1;
551	    for (int j = 0; j < num6; j++)
552	    {
553	        int num7 = width / 2;
554	        int x = centerX + (int)r.Next(-10, 11);
555	        PlaceHill(x - num7 / 2, x + num7 / 2, 0.8, surface, left, width, centerX, windRight);
556	    }
557	}
558	int[] SurfaceFromArea(int startX, int width)
559	{
560	    int half = H / 2;
561	    var arr = new int[width];
562	    for (int i = startX; i < startX + width; i++)
563	    {
564	        if (i < 0 || i >= W) { arr[i - startX] = half + 50; continue; }
565	        bool flag = false; int num2 = 0;
566	        for (int j = 50; j < 50 + half; j++)
567	        {
568	            int ti = j * W + i;
569	            if (act[ti] == 1)
570	            {
571	                if (CLOUDS.Contains(typ[ti])) flag = false;
572	                else if (!flag) { num2 = j; flag = true; }
573	            }
574	            if (!flag) num2 = half + 50;
575	        }
576	        arr[i - startX] = num2;
577	    }
578	    return arr;
579	}
580	void PlaceHill(int startX, int endX, double scale, int[] surface, int left, int width, int centerX, bool windRight)
581	{
582	    int sy0 = surface[startX - (left - 20)], sy1 = surface[endX - (left - 20)];
583	    int midX = (startX + endX) / 2, midY = (sy0 + sy1) / 2 - (int)(35.0 * scale);
584	    int num = (endX - midX) / 4;
585	    int minV = (endX - midX) / 16;
586	    if (!windRight) midX -= (int)r.Next(minV, num + 1);
587	    else midX += (int)r.Next(minV, num + 1);
588	    int aX = 0, aY = (int)(scale * 12.0);
589	    PlaceCurvedLine(startX, sy0, midX, midY, windRight ? (-aX / 2, -aY / 2) : (aX, aY), surface, left, width, centerX);
590	    // line2 锚（cs 源码：WindDirection == Left ? point3 : point2 —— Right 风用 p2，与 line1 相反）
591	    PlaceCurvedLine(midX, midY, endX, sy1, windRight ? (aX, aY) : (-aX / 2, -aY / 2), surface, left, width, centerX);
592	}
593	void PlaceCurvedLine(int sx, int sy, int ex, int ey, (int, int) anchor, int[] surface, int left, int width, int centerX)
594	{
595	    int px = (sx + ex) / 2 + anchor.Item1, py = (sy + ey) / 2 + anchor.Item2;
596	    double step = 0.5 / (double)(ex - sx);
597	    int prevX = -1, prevY = -1;
598	    for (double t = 0.0; t <= 1.0; t += step)
599	    {
600	        double lx = sx + (px - sx) * t, ly = sy + (py - sy) * t;
601	        double rx = px + (ex - px) * t, ry = py + (ey - py) * t;
602	        int cx = (int)(lx + (rx - lx) * t), cy = (int)(ly + (ry - ly) * t);
603	        if (cx == prevX && cy == prevY) continue;
604	        prevX = cx; prevY = cy;
605	        if (cx < 0 || cx >= W) continue;
606	        int num3 = width / 2 - Math.Abs(cx - centerX);
607	        // (int)NaN x86 语义（int.MinValue）——dotnet ARM64 原生给 0，须显式模拟（金标实证）
608	        double sq = Math.Sqrt(num3) * 3.0;
609	        int sqi = double.IsNaN(sq) ? int.MinValue : (int)sq;
610	        int num4 = surface[cx - (left - 20)] + sqi;
611	        for (int i2 = cy - 10; i2 < cy; i2++)
612	        {
613	            if (i2 < 0 || i2 >= H) continue;
614	            int ti = i2 * W + cx;
615	            if (act[ti] == 1 && typ[ti] != 53) { act[ti] = 0; typ[ti] = 0; wal[ti] = 0; liq[ti] = 0; }
616	        }
617	        for (int j2 = cy; j2 < num4; j2++)
618	        {
619	            if (j2 < 0 || j2 >= H) continue;
620	            int ti = j2 * W + cx;
621	            act[ti] = 1; typ[ti] = 53;
622	        }
623	    }
624	}

666	                    typ[l * W + j] = 53;
667	            }
668	            break;
669	        }
670	    }
671	}

1171	{
1172	
1173	    void SpreadGrass(int i, int j)
1174	    {
1175	        if (i < 10 || i >= W - 10 || j < 10 || j >= H - 10) return; // InWorld(i,j,10)
1176	        int ci = j * W + i;
1177	        if (act[ci] == 0 || typ[ci] != 59) return;
1178	        int x0 = Math.Max(0, i - 1), x1 = Math.Min(W, i + 2);
1179	        int y0 = Math.Max(0, j - 1), y1 = Math.Min(H, j + 2);
1180	        bool enclosed = true;
1181	        for (int k = x0; k < x1; k++)
1182	        {
1183	            for (int l = y0; l < y1; l++)
1184	            {
1185	                int wi = l * W + k;
1186	                if (act[wi] == 0 || !IsSolidT(typ[wi])) enclosed = false;
1187	                if (liqT[wi] == 1 && liq[wi] > 0) { enclosed = true; break; } // lava 只 break 内层
1188	            }
1189	        }
1190	        if (enclosed || !CanBeCleared(typ[ci])) return;
1191	        typ[ci] = 60;
1192	        for (int m = x0; m < x1; m++)
1193	            for (int n = y0; n < y1; n++)
1194	            {
1195	                int ni = n * W + m;
1196	                if (act[ni] == 1 && typ[ni] == 59 && grassSpread < 1000)
1197	                {
1198	                    grassSpread++;
1199	                    SpreadGrass(m, n);
1200	                    grassSpread--;
1201	                }
1202	            }
1203	    }
1204	    for (int i = 0; i < W; i++)
1205	        for (int j = 0; j < H; j++)
1206	            if (act[j * W + i] == 1) { grassSpread = 0; SpreadGrass(i, j); }
1207	    // ScanTileColumnAndRemoveClumps（cs:10164-10192 + tileCounter cs:10029-10070）
1208	    const int CMAX = 20;
1209	    int[] cx = new int[CMAX], cy = new int[CMAX];
1210	    int cn = 0;
1211	    void Flood(int x, int y)
1212	    {
1213	        if (cn >= CMAX || x < 5 || x >= W - 5 || y < 5 || y >= H - 5) return;
1214	        int i = y * W + x;
1215	        if (act[i] == 0 || !IsSolidT(typ[i]) || !CanBeCleared(typ[i])) return;
1216	        for (int n = 0; n < cn; n++) if (cx[n] == x && cy[n] == y) return;
1217	        cx[cn] = x; cy[cn] = cn >= 0 ? y : y; cn++;
1218	        Flood(x - 1, y); Flood(x + 1, y); Flood(x, y - 1); Flood(x, y + 1);
1219	    }
1220	    for (int x = 10; x < W - 10; x++)
1221	    {
1222	        int run = 0, runY = 0;
1223	        for (int y = 10; y < H - 10; y++)
1224	        {
1225	            int i = y * W + x;
1226	            if (act[i] == 1 && IsSolidT(typ[i]) && CanBeCleared(typ[i]))
1227	            {
1228	                if (run == 0) runY = y;
1229	                run++;
1230	                continue;
1231	            }
1232	            if (run > 0 && run < CMAX)
1233	            {
1234	                cn = 0;
1235	                Flood(x, runY);
1236	                if (cn < CMAX) for (int n = 0; n < cn; n++) act[cy[n] * W + cx[n]] = 0;
1237	            }
1238	            run = 0;
1239	        }
1240	    }
1241	}

1245	// Sub-B（DesertHive）与 Sub-C（入口 4 形状）转录至 Checkpoint("desertdone")。
1246	// DesertDescription 字段（DesertDescription.cs:73-83；普通世界 num7=0）
1247	int dBC = 0, dBR = 0, dX = 0, dTop = 0, dW = 0, dH = 0, dHiveTop = 0;
1248	int[] SurfFromArea(int startX, int width)
1249	{
1250	    int half = H / 2;
1251	    var arr = new int[width];
1252	    for (int i = 0; i < width; i++)
1253	    {
1254	        int x = startX + i;
1255	        bool flag = false; int y = half + 50;
1256	        if (x >= 0 && x < W)
1257	        {
1258	            for (int j = 50; j < 50 + half; j++)
1259	            {
1260	                int ti = j * W + x;
1261	                if (act[ti] == 1)
1262	                {
1263	                    if (typ[ti] == 189 || typ[ti] == 196 || typ[ti] == 460 || typ[ti] == 717 || typ[ti] == 718 || typ[ti] == 719) flag = false;
1264	                    else if (!flag) { y = j; flag = true; }
1265	                }
1266	                if (!flag) y = half + 50;
1267	            }
1268	        }
1269	        arr[i] = y;
1270	    }
1271	    return arr;
1272	}
1273	(int[], double, int) SurfStats(int[] hs)
1274	{
1275	    long sum = 0; int mn = int.MaxValue, mx = 0;
1276	    foreach (var v in hs) { sum += v; mn = Math.Min(mn, v); mx = Math.Max(mx, v); }
1277	    int cap = worldSurface - 10;
1278	    if (mx > cap) mx = cap;
1279	    return (hs, (double)sum / hs.Length, mx);
1280	}
1281	bool DesertPlaceMound(int x)
1282	{
1283	    double factor = W / 4200.0;
1284	    int blockCols = (int)(80.0 * factor);
1285	    int blockRows = (int)(((double)r.NextDouble() * 0.5 + 1.5) * 170.0 * factor);
1286	    int dw = (int)(4.0 * blockCols), dh = (int)(2.0 * blockRows);
1287	    int ox = x - dw / 2;
1288	    var pre = SurfFromArea(ox - 5, dw + 10);
1289	    var (_, avg, bottom) = SurfStats(pre);
1290	    for (int i = ox; i < ox + dw; i++)
1291	    {
1292	        if (i < 0 || i >= W) continue;
1293	        ushort t = typ[bottom * W + i];
1294	        if (t == 59 || t == 60 || t == 161 || t == 147) return false;
1295	    }
1296	    int topY = (int)(avg + (double)bottom) / 2;
1297	    int hiveTop = topY + (int)r.Next(40, 60);
1298	    int desertH = hiveTop + dh / 2 - topY;
1299	    int moundH = Math.Min(desertH, dh / 2);
1300	    int moundBottom = topY + moundH;
1301	    int sandBottom = topY + desertH;
1302	    int coarse = 0, fine = 0;
1303	    for (int i = -5; i < dw + 5; i++)
1304	    {
1305	        int cx = ox + i;
1306	        double tv = Math.Abs((i + 5) / (double)(dw + 10)) * 2.0 - 1.0;
1307	        tv = Math.Clamp(tv, -1.0, 1.0);
1308	        if (i % 3 == 0) { coarse += (int)r.Next(-1, 2); coarse = Math.Clamp(coarse, -10, 10); }
1309	        fine += (int)r.Next(-1, 2); fine = Math.Clamp(fine, -10, 10);
1310	        double prof = Math.Sqrt(1.0 - tv * tv * tv * tv);
1311	        int surfY = moundBottom - (int)(prof * moundH) + coarse;
1312	        if (Math.Abs(tv) < 1.0 && cx >= 0 && cx < W)
1313	        {
1314	            double ramp = (Math.Abs(tv) - 0.5) / 0.3;
1315	            int val = Math.Min(10 + (int)((double)topY - ramp * ramp * ramp * 20.0) + fine, surfY);
1316	            int si = Math.Clamp(i + 5, 0, pre.Length - 1);
1317	            for (int j = pre[si] - 1; j < val; j++)
1318	            {
1319	                if (j < 0 || j >= H) continue;
1320	                act[j * W + cx] = 0;
1321	                wal[j * W + cx] = 0;
1322	            }
1323	        }
1324	        for (int y = surfY + (sandBottom - surfY) - 1; y >= surfY; y--)
1325	        {
1326	            if (y < 0 || y >= H) continue;
1327	            typ[y * W + cx] = 53; act[y * W + cx] = 1; liq[y * W + cx] = 0;
1328	        }
1329	    }
1330	    dBC = blockCols; dBR = blockRows; dX = ox; dTop = topY; dW = dw; dH = dh; dHiveTop = hiveTop;
1331	    return true;
1332	}
1333	{
1334	    int halfW = W / 2;
1335	    int side = dungeonRight ? 1 : -1;
1336	    int tries = 0, flips = 0; bool skipCheck = false;
1337	    int num4 = (int)r.Next(0, halfW) / 8 + halfW / 8;
1338	    int dx0 = halfW + num4 * -side;
1339	    while (!DesertPlaceMound(dx0))
1340	    {
1341	        num4 = (int)r.Next(0, halfW) / 2 + halfW / 8 + (int)r.Next(0, tries / 12);
1342	        dx0 = halfW + num4 * -side;
1343	        if (++tries > W / 4)
1344	        {
1345	            side = -side;
1346	            tries = 0;
1347	            if (++flips >= 2) skipCheck = true;
1348	        }
1349	        if (skipCheck) { DesertPlaceMound(dx0); break; }
1350	    }
1351	}

1363	void DesertChambers()
1364	{
1365	    int num = (dX + dW / 2) + (int)r.Next(-40, 41);       // Desert.Center.X + Next(-40,41)
1366	    int px = num, py = DSurf(num);
1367	    var shapeData = new HashSet<long>();
1368	    var data = new HashSet<long>();
1369	    int ox = px, oy = py + 2;
1370	    GW.GenCircle(ox, oy, 24, 12, GW.Chain(new GW.BlotchesA(), GW.Out(new GW.SetTileA(53), shapeData)));
1371	    int hnum = dHiveTop - py;                             // Hive.Top - position.Y
1372	    int num2 = (int)r.Next(2) != 0 ? 1 : -1;
1373	    var list = new List<(double x, double y, double dir)> { (px + -num2 * 26, (double)(py - 8), (double)num2) };
1374	    int num3 = (int)r.Next(2, 4);
1375	    for (int i = 0; i < num3; i++)
1376	    {
1377	        int num4 = (int)((double)(i + 1) / (double)num3 * (double)hnum) + (int)r.Next(-8, 9);
1378	        int num5 = num2 * (int)r.Next(20, 41);
1379	        int num6 = (int)r.Next(18, 29);
1380	        GW.GenCircle(px, py, num6 / 2, 3, GW.Chain(new GW.OffsetA(num5, num4), new GW.BlotchesA(), GW.Out(new GW.ClearA(), data), new GW.PlaceWallA(187)));
1381	        list.Add((num5 + num6 / 2 * -num2 + px, (double)(num4 + py), (double)(-num2)));
1382	        num2 *= -1;
1383	    }
1384	    GW.GenOutline(px, py, data, GW.Chain(new GW.ExpandA(1), new GW.OnlyTilesA(53), new GW.SetTileA(397), new GW.PlaceWallA(187)));
1385	    GA pairAct = GW.Chain(new GW.IsSolidA(), new GW.BlotchesA(), new GW.ClearA(), new GW.ExpandA(1), new GW.PlaceWallA(187), new GW.OnlyTilesA(53), new GW.SetTileA(397));
1386	    for (int j = 1; j < list.Count; j++)
1387	    {
1388	        var pa = list[j - 1]; var pb = list[j];
1389	        double connLen = Math.Abs(pb.x - pa.x) * 1.5;
1390	        for (double t = 0.0; t <= 1.0; t += 0.02)
1391	        {
1392	            double ax = pa.x + pa.dir * connLen * t, ay = pa.y;
1393	            double bx = pb.x + pb.dir * connLen * (1.0 - t), by = pb.y;
1394	            double cx = pa.x + (pb.x - pa.x) * t, cy = pa.y + (pb.y - pa.y) * t;   // Lerp(p0,p1,t)
1395	            double dx = ax + (cx - ax) * t, dy = ay + (cy - ay) * t;                // Lerp(v,v3,t)
1396	            double ex = cx + (bx - cx) * t, ey = cy + (by - cy) * t;                // Lerp(v3,v2,t)
1397	            double fx = dx + (ex - dx) * t, fy = dy + (ey - dy) * t;                // Lerp(v4,v5,t)
1398	            GW.GenRectWH((int)fx, (int)fy, 2, 4, pairAct);
1399	        }
1400	    }
1401	    GW.GenRect(ox, oy, -29, -12, 29, 0, GW.Chain(new GW.NotInShapeA(shapeData), new GW.ExpandA(1), new GW.PlaceWallA(0)));
1402	}

1404	// ---- AnthillEntrance.cs 1:1 ----
1405	void DesertAnthill()
1406	{
1407	    int num = (int)r.Next(2, 4);
1408	    for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
1409	    {
1410	        int holeRadius = (int)r.Next(15, 18);
1411	        int num2 = (int)((double)(i + 1) / (double)(num + 1) * (double)(dW + 10)) + dX;  // Surface.Width=dW+10
1412	        DesertAnthillAt(num2, DSurf(num2), holeRadius);
1413	    }
1414	}
1415	void DesertAnthillAt(int px, int py, int holeRadius)
1416	{
1417	    var data = new HashSet<long>();
1418	    int ox = px, oy = py + 6;
1419	    GW.GenTail(ox, oy, holeRadius * 2, 0.0, -(double)holeRadius * 1.5, GW.Out(new GW.SetTileA(53), data));
1420	    GA pairA = GW.Chain(new GW.BlotchesA(), new GW.IsSolidA(), new GW.ClearA(), new GW.PlaceWallA(187));
1421	    GA pairB = GW.Chain(new GW.IsSolidA(), new GW.ClearA(), new GW.PlaceWallA(187));
1422	    GA pairC = GW.Chain(new GW.IsSolidA(), new GW.SetTileA(397), new GW.PlaceWallA(187));
1423	    GA pairD = GW.Chain(new GW.SkipWallsA(187), new GW.SetTileA(53));
1424	    GA pairE = new GW.PlaceWallA(187);
1425	    int num = px;
1426	    for (int i = py - holeRadius - 3; i < dHiveTop + (py - dTop) * 2 + 12; i++)
1427	    {
1428	        GW.GenRectWH(num, i, 1, 1, i < py ? pairB : pairA);
1429	        GW.GenCircle(num, i, 2, 3, pairC);
1430	        if (i % 3 == 0 && i >= py)
1431	        {
1432	            num += (int)r.Next(-1, 2);
1433	            GW.GenRectWH(num, i, 1, 1, pairA);
1434	            if (i >= py + 5)
1435	            {
1436	                GW.GenCircle(num, i, holeRadius, 3, pairD);
1437	                GW.GenCircle(num, i, holeRadius - 2, 3, pairE);
1438	            }
1439	            GW.GenCircle(num, i, 2, 3, pairC);
1440	        }
1441	    }
1442	    GW.GenCircle(ox, oy - (int)((double)holeRadius * 1.5) + 3, holeRadius / 2, holeRadius / 3, GW.Chain(new GW.ClearTileA(), new GW.ExpandA(1), new GW.PlaceWallA(0)));
1443	    // All(data)+Smooth：仅坡面/半砖——哈希无关，两侧一致省略
1444	}

1446	// ---- LarvaHoleEntrance.cs 1:1 ----
1447	void DesertLarvaHole()
1448	{
1449	    int num = (int)r.Next(2, 4);
1450	    for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
1451	    {
1452	        int holeRadius = (int)r.Next(13, 16);
1453	        int num2 = (int)((double)(i + 1) / (double)(num + 1) * (double)(dW + 10)) + dX;
1454	        DesertLarvaAt(num2, DSurf(num2), holeRadius);
1455	    }
1456	}
1457	void DesertLarvaAt(int px, int py, int holeRadius)
1458	{
1459	    var data = new HashSet<long>();
1460	    GW.GenRect(px, py, -holeRadius, -holeRadius * 2, holeRadius, 0, GW.Out(new GW.ClearA(), data));
1461	    GW.GenTail(px, py, holeRadius * 2, 0.0, (double)holeRadius * 1.5, GW.Out(new GW.ClearA(), data));
1462	    // All(data)+Offset(0,1)+Expand(1)+IsSolid+Smooth：仅坡面——哈希无关，省略
1463	    GA pairA = GW.Chain(new GW.BlotchesA(), new GW.IsSolidA(), new GW.ClearA(), new GW.PlaceWallA(187));
1464	    GA pairB = GW.Chain(new GW.IsSolidA(), new GW.SetTileA(397), new GW.PlaceWallA(187));
1465	    int num = px;
1466	    for (int i = py + (int)((double)holeRadius * 1.5); i < dHiveTop + (py - dTop) * 2 + 12; i++)
1467	    {
1468	        GW.GenRectWH(num, i, 1, 1, pairA);
1469	        GW.GenCircle(num, i, 2, 3, pairB);
1470	        if (i % 3 == 0)
1471	        {
1472	            num += (int)r.Next(-1, 2);
1473	            GW.GenRectWH(num, i, 1, 1, pairA);
1474	            GW.GenCircle(num, i, 2, 3, pairB);
1475	        }
1476	    }
1477	    GW.GenAll(px, py + 2, data, new GW.PlaceWallA(0));
1478	}

1480	// ---- PitEntrance.cs 1:1（零掷骰）----
1481	double PitSmootherStep(double delta)
1482	{
1483	    delta = Math.Clamp(delta, 0.0, 1.0);
1484	    return 1.0 - Math.Cos(delta * 3.1415927410125732) * 0.5 - 0.5;
1485	}
1486	double PitScale(double yProgress)
1487	    => yProgress < 0.6 ? 1.0 : (1.0 - PitSmootherStep((yProgress - 0.6) / 0.4)) * 0.5 + 0.5;
1488	void DesertPit()
1489	{
1490	    int holeRadius = (int)r.Next(6, 9);
1491	    int px = dX + dW / 2, py = DSurf(dX + dW / 2);        // CombinedArea.Center.X
1492	    for (int i = -holeRadius - 3; i < holeRadius + 3; i++)
1493	    {
1494	        int cx = i + px;
1495	        if (cx < 0 || cx >= W) continue;
1496	        int sj = DSurf(cx);
1497	        for (int j = sj; j <= dHiveTop + 10; j++)
1498	        {
1499	            if (j < 0 || j >= H) continue;
1500	            double value = Math.Clamp((double)(j - sj) / (double)(dHiveTop - dTop), 0.0, 1.0);
1501	            int num3 = (int)(PitScale(value) * (double)holeRadius);
1502	            int ti = j * W + cx;
1503	            if (Math.Abs(i) < num3) { act[ti] = 0; typ[ti] = 0; wal[ti] = 0; liq[ti] = 0; }        // ClearEverything
1504	            else if (Math.Abs(i) < num3 + 3 && value > 0.35) { typ[ti] = 397; act[ti] = 1; liq[ti] = 0; }  // ResetToType(397)
1505	            double num4 = Math.Abs((double)i / (double)holeRadius);
1506	            num4 *= num4;
1507	            if (Math.Abs(i) < num3 + 3 && (double)(j - py) > 15.0 - 3.0 * num4) wal[ti] = 187;
1508	        }
1509	    }
1510	    holeRadius += 4;
1511	    for (int k = -holeRadius; k < holeRadius; k++)
1512	    {
1513	        int cx = k + px;
1514	        if (cx < 0 || cx >= W) continue;
1515	        int num5 = Math.Min(10, (holeRadius - Math.Abs(k)) * (holeRadius - Math.Abs(k)));
1516	        for (int l = 0; l < num5; l++)
1517	        {
1518	            int y = l + DSurf(cx);
1519	            if (y < 0 || y >= H) continue;
1520	            int ti = y * W + cx;
1521	            act[ti] = 0; typ[ti] = 0; wal[ti] = 0; liq[ti] = 0;
1522	        }
1523	    }
1524	}
1525	
1526	if ((double)r.NextDouble() <= 0.5)
1527	{
1528	    switch ((int)r.Next(4))
1529	    {
1530	        case 0: DesertChambers(); break;
1531	        case 1: DesertAnthill(); break;
1532	        case 2: DesertLarvaHole(); break;
1533	        case 3: DesertPit(); break;
1534	    }
1535	}
1536	Checkpoint("desertentrance");
1537	
1538	// ================= Desert Sub-B Hive（DesertHive.cs 1:1）=================
1539	{
1540	    // ---- ClusterGroup.Generate（椭圆散点→深度2 DFS→AttemptClaim→≥4 重组）----
1541	    var dhMap = new bool[dBC, dBR];
1542	    {
1543	        int e1 = dBC / 2 - 1, e2 = dBR / 2 - 1;
1544	        int e3 = (e1 + 1) * (e1 + 1);
1545	        for (int ei = 0; ei <= e2 * 2; ei++)      // 原版 i ∈ [0, 2*(H/2-1)]——末行不散点！
1546	        {
1547	            double e4 = (double)e1 / (double)e2 * (double)(ei - e2);
1548	            int e5 = Math.Min(e1, (int)Math.Sqrt((double)e3 - e4 * e4));
1549	            for (int ej = e1 - e5; ej <= e1 + e5; ej++)
1550	                dhMap[ej, ei] = (int)r.Next(2) == 0;
1551	        }
1552	    }
1553	    var dhPCs = new List<List<(int x, int y)>>();
1554	    void dhSearch(int x, int y, int level, List<(int x, int y)> pc)
1555	    {
1556	        pc.Add((x, y));
1557	        dhMap[x, y] = false;
1558	        level--;
1559	        if (level == -1) return;
1560	        if (x > 0 && dhMap[x - 1, y]) dhSearch(x - 1, y, level, pc);
1561	        if (x < dBC - 1 && dhMap[x + 1, y]) dhSearch(x + 1, y, level, pc);
1562	        if (y > 0 && dhMap[x, y - 1]) dhSearch(x, y - 1, level, pc);
1563	        if (y < dBR - 1 && dhMap[x, y + 1]) dhSearch(x, y + 1, level, pc);
1564	    }
1565	    for (int sk = 0; sk < dBC; sk++)
1566	        for (int sl = 0; sl < dBR; sl++)
1567	            if (dhMap[sk, sl] && (int)r.Next(2) == 0)
1568	            {
1569	                var spc = new List<(int x, int y)>();
1570	                dhSearch(sk, sl, 2, spc);
1571	                if (spc.Count > 2) dhPCs.Add(spc);
1572	            }
1573	    var dhIdx = new int[dBC, dBR];
1574	    for (int fm = 0; fm < dBC; fm++)
1575	        for (int fn = 0; fn < dBR; fn++)
1576	            dhIdx[fm, fn] = -1;
1577	    for (int fc = 0; fc < dhPCs.Count; fc++)
1578	        foreach (var fp in dhPCs[fc]) dhIdx[fp.x, fp.y] = fc;
1579	    void dhClaim(int x, int y, int index)
1580	    {
1581	        int cn = dhIdx[x, y];
1582	        if (cn == -1 || cn == index) return;
1583	        int cv = (int)r.Next(2) == 0 ? -1 : index;
1584	        foreach (var cp in dhPCs[cn]) dhIdx[cp.x, cp.y] = cv;
1585	    }
1586	    for (int gc = 0; gc < dhPCs.Count; gc++)
1587	        foreach (var gp in dhPCs[gc])
1588	        {
1589	            if (dhIdx[gp.x, gp.y] == -1) break;
1590	            int gi = dhIdx[gp.x, gp.y];
1591	            if (gp.x > 0) dhClaim(gp.x - 1, gp.y, gi);
1592	            if (gp.x < dBC - 1) dhClaim(gp.x + 1, gp.y, gi);
1593	            if (gp.y > 0) dhClaim(gp.x, gp.y - 1, gi);
1594	            if (gp.y < dBR - 1) dhClaim(gp.x, gp.y + 1, gi);
1595	        }
1596	    {
1597	        foreach (var pc0 in dhPCs) pc0.Clear();
1598	        for (int rx = 0; rx < dBC; rx++)
1599	            for (int ry = 0; ry < dBR; ry++)
1600	                if (dhIdx[rx, ry] != -1) dhPCs[dhIdx[rx, ry]].Add((rx, ry));
1601	        foreach (var pc1 in dhPCs)
1602	            if (pc1.Count < 4) pc1.Clear();
1603	    }
1604	    var dhClusters = new List<List<(double x, double y)>>();
1605	    foreach (var pc2 in dhPCs)
1606	    {
1607	        if (pc2.Count <= 0) continue;
1608	        var cl = new List<(double x, double y)>();
1609	        foreach (var bp in pc2)
1610	            cl.Add((bp.x + ((double)r.NextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.5, bp.y + ((double)r.NextDouble() - 0.5) * 0.5));
1611	        dhClusters.Add(cl);
1612	    }
1613	
1614	    // ---- PlaceClustersArea（场强分层；shell 类型骰与 0.25 门用 FastRandom，非 genRand！）----
1615	    var dhFr = new FR((ulong)(long)seed).Mod(57005UL);
1616	    for (int hi = dX - 20; hi < dX + dW + 20; hi++)
1617	    {
1618	        for (int hj = dHiveTop - 20; hj < dHiveTop + dH + 20; hj++)
1619	        {
1620	            if (hi < 1 || hi >= W - 1 || hj < 1 || hj >= H - 1) continue;   // InWorld(i,j,1)
1621	            int hti = hj * W + hi;
1622	            double f1 = 0.0; int f2 = -1; double f3 = 0.0;
1623	            ushort hType = 53;
1624	            if (dhFr.Next(3) == 0) hType = 397;
1625	            int n4 = hi - dX, n5 = hj - dHiveTop;
1626	            double vx = ((double)n4 - 2.0) / (double)dW * (double)dBC;   // (p-BlockScale/2)/Hive*Cluster
1627	            double vy = ((double)n5 - 1.0) / (double)dH * (double)dBR;
1628	            for (int hk = 0; hk < dhClusters.Count; hk++)
1629	            {
1630	                var hcl = dhClusters[hk];
1631	                if (Math.Abs(hcl[0].x - vx) > 10.0 || Math.Abs(hcl[0].y - vy) > 10.0) continue;
1632	                double f6 = 0.0;
1633	                foreach (var hb in hcl)
1634	                {
1635	                    double ddx = hb.x - vx, ddy = hb.y - vy;
1636	                    f6 += 1.0 / (ddx * ddx + ddy * ddy);
1637	                }
1638	                if (f6 > f1) { if (f1 > f3) f3 = f1; f1 = f6; f2 = hk; }
1639	                else if (f6 > f3) f3 = f6;
1640	            }
1641	            double f7 = f1 + f3;
1642	            double hex = ((double)n4 - 2.0) / (double)dW * 2.0 - 1.0;
1643	            double hey = ((double)n5 - 1.0) / (double)dH * 2.0 - 1.0;
1644	            bool hEdge = Math.Sqrt(hex * hex + hey * hey) >= 0.8;        // Vector2D.Length()
1645	            bool hFlag2 = true;
1646	            if (f7 > 3.5)
1647	            {
1648	                act[hti] = 0; typ[hti] = 0; wal[hti] = 0; liq[hti] = 0;  // ClearEverything
1649	                wal[hti] = 187;
1650	                if (f2 % 15 == 2) { typ[hti] = 404; act[hti] = 1; liq[hti] = 0; }  // ResetToType
1651	            }
1652	            else if (f7 > 1.8)
1653	            {
1654	                wal[hti] = 187;
1655	                if ((double)hj < (double)worldSurface) liq[hti] = 0;     // else lava()：liq 量不变
1656	                else liqT[hti] = 1;                                      // ★lava 类型位（JS liquidType=2）
1657	                if (!hEdge || act[hti] != 0) { typ[hti] = 396; act[hti] = 1; liq[hti] = 0; }
1658	            }
1659	            else if (f7 > 0.7 || !hEdge)
1660	            {
1661	                wal[hti] = 216;
1662	                liq[hti] = 0;
1663	                if (!hEdge || act[hti] != 0) { typ[hti] = hType; act[hti] = 1; liq[hti] = 0; }
1664	            }
1665	            else if (f7 > 0.25)
1666	            {
1667	                var hFr2 = dhFr.ModXY(n4, n5);
1668	                double f8 = (f7 - 0.25) / 0.45;
1669	                if (hFr2.NextDouble() < f8)
1670	                {
1671	                    wal[hti] = 187;
1672	                    if ((double)hj < (double)worldSurface) liq[hti] = 0;
1673	                    else liqT[hti] = 1;                                  // ★lava 类型位
1674	                    if (act[hti] != 0) { typ[hti] = hType; act[hti] = 1; liq[hti] = 0; }
1675	                }
1676	            }
1677	            else hFlag2 = false;
1678	            // hFlag2 → UpdateDesertHiveBounds（纯边界追踪，无哈希效应）
1679	        }
1680	    }
1681	
1682	    // ---- AddTileVariance（DesertHive.cs:413-499）----
1683	    for (int vi = -20; vi < dW + 20; vi++)
1684	    {
1685	        for (int vj = -20; vj < dH + 20; vj++)
1686	        {
1687	            int vx2 = vi + dX, vy2 = vj + dHiveTop;
1688	            if (vx2 < 1 || vx2 >= W - 1 || vy2 < 1 || vy2 >= H - 1) continue;
1689	            int vti = vy2 * W + vx2;
1690	            bool vs1 = vy2 + 1 < H && act[(vy2 + 1) * W + vx2] != 0 && SOLID.Contains(typ[(vy2 + 1) * W + vx2]);
1691	            bool vs2 = vy2 + 2 < H && act[(vy2 + 2) * W + vx2] != 0 && SOLID.Contains(typ[(vy2 + 2) * W + vx2]);
1692	            if (typ[vti] == 53 && (!vs1 || !vs2)) typ[vti] = 397;
1693	        }
1694	    }
1695	    for (int wk = -20; wk < dW + 20; wk++)
1696	    {
1697	        for (int wl = -20; wl < dH + 20; wl++)
1698	        {
1699	            int wx = wk + dX, wy = wl + dHiveTop;
1700	            if (wx < 5 || wx >= W - 5 || wy < 5 || wy >= H - 5) continue;
1701	            int wti = wy * W + wx;
1702	            if (act[wti] == 0 || typ[wti] != 396) continue;
1703	            bool wFlag = true;
1704	            for (int wn = -1; wn >= -3; wn--)
1705	                if (wy + wn >= 0 && (act[(wy + wn) * W + wx] != 0 || act[(wy + wn) * W + wx + 1] != 0)) { wFlag = false; break; }
1706	            bool wFlag2 = true;
1707	            for (int wm = 1; wm <= 3; wm++)
1708	                if (wy + wm < H && (act[(wy + wm) * W + wx] != 0 || act[(wy + wm) * W + wx + 1] != 0)) { wFlag2 = false; break; }
1709	            // 装饰（485/751/484/165/187）两侧一致省略放置，掷骰照原序消耗保持流对齐
1710	            if (wFlag && (int)r.Next(20) == 0) { int wStyle = (int)r.Next(4); if ((int)r.Next(30) == 0) wStyle = 0; }
1711	            else if (wFlag && (int)r.Next(5) == 0) { }
1712	            else if ((wFlag ^ wFlag2) && (int)r.Next(5) == 0) { }
1713	            else if (wFlag && (int)r.Next(5) == 0) { }
1714	        }
1715	    }
1716	}
1717	Checkpoint("deserthive");
1718	Checkpoint("desertdone");   // CleanupArea 仅 SquareWallFrame/TileFrame——无哈希效应

1720	// ================= Pass 20 GlowingMushroomPatches（cs:12581-12709 + ShroomPatch cs:80540 + SpreadGrass cs:75210）=================
1721	void SpreadGrassGen(int gi, int gj, int gdirt, int ggrass)
1722	{
1723	    if (gi < 10 || gi >= W - 10 || gj < 10 || gj >= H - 10) return;   // InWorld(i,j,10)
1724	    int gi2 = gj * W + gi;
1725	    if (act[gi2] == 0 || typ[gi2] != gdirt) return;
1726	    int gx0 = Math.Max(0, gi - 1), gx1 = Math.Min(W, gi + 2);
1727	    int gy0 = Math.Max(0, gj - 1), gy1 = Math.Min(H, gj + 2);
1728	    bool gEnclosed = true;
1729	    for (int gk = gx0; gk < gx1; gk++)
1730	        for (int gl = gy0; gl < gy1; gl++)
1731	        {
1732	            int gti = gl * W + gk;
1733	            if (act[gti] == 0 || !SOLID.Contains(typ[gti])) gEnclosed = false;
1734	            if (liqT[gti] == 1 && liq[gti] > 0) { gEnclosed = true; break; }   // lava
1735	        }
1736	    if (gEnclosed || NOT_CLEAR.Contains(typ[gi2])) return;    // CanBeClearedDuringGeneration
1737	    typ[gi2] = (ushort)ggrass;
1738	}
1739	void ShroomPatch(int si, int sj)
1740	{
1741	    double sn = (int)r.Next(80, 100);
1742	    double sn2 = (int)r.Next(20, 26);
1743	    double sn3 = W / 4200.0;
1744	    sn *= sn3; sn2 *= sn3;
1745	    double sn4 = sn2 - 1.0, sn5 = sn;
1746	    double svx = si, svy = sj - sn2 * 0.3;
1747	    double sv2x = (int)r.Next(-100, 101) * 0.005, sv2y = (int)r.Next(-200, -100) * 0.005;
1748	    while (sn > 0.0 && sn2 > 0.0)
1749	    {
1750	        sn -= (int)r.Next(3);
1751	        sn2 -= 1.0;
1752	        int sx0 = Math.Max(0, (int)(svx - sn * 0.5)), sx1 = Math.Min(W, (int)(svx + sn * 0.5));
1753	        int sy0 = Math.Max(0, (int)(svy - sn * 0.5)), sy1 = Math.Min(H, (int)(svy + sn * 0.5));
1754	        sn5 = sn * (int)r.Next(80, 120) * 0.01;
1755	        for (int sk = sx0; sk < sx1; sk++)
1756	            for (int sl = sy0; sl < sy1; sl++)
1757	            {
1758	                double sd0 = Math.Abs((double)sk - svx);
1759	                double sd1 = Math.Abs(((double)sl - svy) * 2.3);
1760	                double sd2 = Math.Sqrt(sd0 * sd0 + sd1 * sd1);
1761	                int sti = sl * W + sk;
1762	                if (sd2 < sn5 * 0.8 && liqT[sti] == 1) liq[sti] = 0;    // tile.lava()
1763	                if (sd2 < sn5 * 0.2 && (double)sl < svy)
1764	                {
1765	                    act[sti] = 0;
1766	                    if (wal[sti] > 0) wal[sti] = 80;
1767	                }
1768	                else if (sd2 < sn5 * 0.4 * (0.95 + (double)r.NextDouble() * 0.1))
1769	                {
1770	                    typ[sti] = 59;
1771	                    if (sn2 == sn4 && (double)sl > svy) act[sti] = 1;
1772	                    if (wal[sti] > 0) wal[sti] = 80;
1773	                }
1774	            }
1775	        svx += sv2x; svy += sv2y;
1776	        svx += sv2x;                                  // 原版 val+=val2 后再 val.X+=val2.X（X 双倍步进）
1777	        sv2x += (int)r.Next(-100, 110) * 0.005;
1778	        sv2y -= (int)r.Next(110) * 0.005;
1779	        if (sv2x > -0.5 && sv2x < 0.5) sv2x = sv2x < 0.0 ? -0.5 : 0.5;
1780	        if (sv2x > 0.5) sv2x = 0.5;
1781	        if (sv2x < -0.5) sv2x = -0.5;
1782	        if (sv2y > 0.5) sv2y = 0.5;
1783	        if (sv2y < -0.5) sv2y = -0.5;
1784	        for (int sm = 0; sm < 2; sm++)
1785	        {
1786	            int s13 = (int)svx + (int)r.Next(-20, 20);
1787	            int s14 = (int)svy + (int)r.Next(0, 20);
1788	            while (s13 >= 0 && s13 < W && s14 >= 0 && s14 < H && act[s14 * W + s13] == 0 && typ[s14 * W + s13] != 59)
1789	            { s13 = (int)svx + (int)r.Next(-20, 20); s14 = (int)svy + (int)r.Next(0, 20); }
1790	            TileRunner(s13, s14, (int)r.Next(10, 20), (int)r.Next(10, 20), 59, false, 0.0, 2.0, true);
1791	        }
1792	    }
1793	}
1794	{
1795	    double mnCount = W / 700.0;
1796	    if (mnCount > 50) mnCount = 50;                  // GenVars.maxMushroomBiomes = 50
1797	    var mushPts = new List<(int x, int y)>();
1798	    int mushN = 0;
1799	    // UndergroundDesertLocation 见顶级 dsX/dsY/dsR/dsB（沙漠段落盘后回填）
1800	    for (int mk = 0; (double)mk < mnCount; mk++)
1801	    {
1802	        int m2 = 0; bool mflag = true;
1803	        while (mflag)
1804	        {
1805	            int m3 = (int)r.Next((int)(W * 0.2), (int)(W * 0.8));
1806	            if (m2 > W / 4) m3 = (int)r.Next((int)(W * 0.025), (int)(W * 0.975));
1807	            int m4 = (int)r.Next(rockLayer + 50, H - 300);   // Main.rockLayer
1808	            mflag = false;
1809	            for (int ml = m3 - 100; ml < m3 + 100; ml += 3)
1810	                for (int mm = m4 - 100; mm < m4 + 100; mm += 3)
1811	                {
1812	                    if (ml >= 0 && ml < W && mm >= 0 && mm < H)
1813	                    {
1814	                        int mti = mm * W + ml;
1815	                        if (act[mti] != 0)
1816	                        {
1817	                            ushort mtt = typ[mti];
1818	                            if (mtt == 147 || mtt == 161 || mtt == 162 || mtt == 60 || mtt == 368 || mtt == 367) { mflag = true; break; }
1819	                        }
1820	                        if (ml >= dsX && ml < dsR && mm >= dsY && mm < dsB) { mflag = true; break; }
1821	                    }
1822	                    else { mflag = true; break; }
1823	                }
1824	            if (!mflag)
1825	                for (int mp = 0; mp < mushN; mp++)
1826	                    if (Math.Sqrt((double)((mushPts[mp].x - m3) * (mushPts[mp].x - m3) + (mushPts[mp].y - m4) * (mushPts[mp].y - m4))) < 500.0) mflag = true;
1827	            if (!mflag && mushN < 50)
1828	            {
1829	                ShroomPatch(m3, m4);
1830	                for (int mq = 0; mq < 5; mq++) ShroomPatch(m3 + (int)r.Next(-40, 41), m4 + (int)r.Next(-40, 41));
1831	                mushPts.Add((m3, m4)); mushN++;
1832	            }
1833	            m2++;
1834	            if (m2 > W / 2) break;
1835	        }
1836	    }
1837	    // SpreadGrass 扫描（cs:12721-12730 普通世界分支；InWorld(i,j,50)）
1838	    for (int sgx = 0; sgx < W; sgx++)
1839	        for (int sgy = worldSurface; sgy < H; sgy++)
1840	        {
1841	            int sgti = sgy * W + sgx;
1842	            if (sgx >= 50 && sgx < W - 50 && sgy >= 50 && sgy < H - 50 && act[sgti] != 0)
1843	                SpreadGrassGen(sgx, sgy, 59, 70);
1844	        }
1845	    // 蘑菇草饰面第二循环（cs:12731-12769）：KillTile/PlaceTile(59)/Next(4) 散点
1846	    for (int h2x = 0; h2x < W; h2x++)
1847	        for (int h2y = worldSurface; h2y < H; h2y++)
1848	        {
1849	            int h2ti = h2y * W + h2x;
1850	            if (act[h2ti] == 0 || typ[h2ti] != 70) continue;
1851	            for (int ix = h2x - 1; ix <= h2x + 1; ix++)
1852	                for (int iy = h2y - 1; iy <= h2y + 1; iy++)
1853	                {
1854	                    if (ix < 0 || ix >= W || iy < 0 || iy >= H) continue;
1855	                    int iti = iy * W + ix;
1856	                    bool iwL = ix > 0 && act[iy * W + ix - 1] != 0, iwR = ix < W - 1 && act[iy * W + ix + 1] != 0;
1857	                    bool iwU = iy > 0 && act[(iy - 1) * W + ix] != 0, iwD = iy < H - 1 && act[(iy + 1) * W + ix] != 0;
1858	                    if (act[iti] != 0)
1859	                    {
1860	                        if (!iwL && !iwR) act[iti] = 0;                 // KillTile（生成期无掉落无 RNG）
1861	                        else if (!iwU && !iwD) act[iti] = 0;
1862	                    }
1863	                    else if (iwL && iwR)
1864	                    {
1865	                        typ[iti] = 59; act[iti] = 1;                    // PlaceTile(59) 默认分支
1866	                        if (ix > 0 && typ[h2y * W + ix - 1] == 70) typ[h2y * W + ix - 1] = 59;
1867	                        if (ix < W - 1 && typ[h2y * W + ix + 1] == 70) typ[h2y * W + ix + 1] = 59;
1868	                    }
1869	                    else if (iwU && iwD)
1870	                    {
1871	                        typ[iti] = 59; act[iti] = 1;
1872	                        if (h2y > 0 && typ[(h2y - 1) * W + h2x] == 70) typ[(h2y - 1) * W + h2x] = 59;
1873	                        if (h2y < H - 1 && typ[(h2y + 1) * W + h2x] == 70) typ[(h2y + 1) * W + h2x] = 59;
1874	                    }
1875	                }
1876	            if ((int)r.Next(4) == 0)
1877	            {
1878	                int scx = h2x + (int)r.Next(-20, 21), scy = h2y + (int)r.Next(-20, 21);
1879	                if (scx >= 0 && scx < W && scy >= 0 && scy < H && typ[scy * W + scx] == 59) typ[scy * W + scx] = 70;
1880	            }
1881	        }
1882	}
1883	Checkpoint("mushroom");

1885	// ================= Pass 21/22 共用：BiomeTileCheck（cs:10949）+ PlaceTight/Stalactite（cs:38329/38353）=================
1886	bool BiomeTileCheck(int bx, int by)
1887	{
1888	    for (int i = bx - 50; i <= bx + 50; i++)
1889	        for (int j = by - 50; j <= by + 50; j++)
1890	        {
1891	            if (i < 0 || i >= W || j < 0 || j >= H) continue;
1892	            int ti = j * W + i;
1893	            if (act[ti] != 0)
1894	            {
1895	                ushort t = typ[ti];
1896	                if (t == 368 || t == 367 || t == 147 || t == 161 || t == 162 || t == 70 || t == 72 || t == 396 || t == 397) return true;
1897	            }
1898	            if (wal[ti] == 187 || wal[ti] == 216) return true;
1899	        }
1900	    return false;
1901	}
1902	bool SolidTileGen(int sx, int sy) => sx >= 0 && sx < W && sy >= 0 && sy < H && act[sy * W + sx] != 0 && SOLID.Contains(typ[sy * W + sx]);
1903	void PlaceStalactite(int x, int y, bool preferSmall, int variation)   // PlaceUncheckedStalactite 哈希通道语义
1904	{
1905	    variation = Math.Clamp(variation, 0, 2);
1906	    void Put(int ax, int ay) { if (ax >= 0 && ax < W && ay >= 0 && ay < H) { typ[ay * W + ax] = 165; act[ay * W + ax] = 1; } }
1907	    if (SolidTileGen(x, y - 1) && y >= 0 && y < H && act[y * W + x] == 0 && y + 1 >= 0 && y + 1 < H && act[(y + 1) * W + x] == 0)
1908	    {
1909	        ushort ab = y - 1 >= 0 ? typ[(y - 1) * W + x] : (ushort)0;
1910	        bool rockFam = ab == 1 || ab == 117 || ab == 25 || ab == 203;   // tileMoss 此阶段无
1911	        if (ab == 147 || ab == 161 || ab == 163 || ab == 164 || ab == 200) { if (preferSmall) Put(x, y); else { Put(x, y); Put(x, y + 1); } }
1912	        if (rockFam) { if (preferSmall) Put(x, y); else { Put(x, y); Put(x, y + 1); } }
1913	        if (ab == 225) Put(x, y);
1914	        if (ab == 396 || ab == 397) { if (preferSmall) Put(x, y); else { Put(x, y); Put(x, y + 1); } }
1915	        if (ab == 368) { if (preferSmall) Put(x, y); else { Put(x, y); Put(x, y + 1); } }
1916	        if (ab == 367) { if (preferSmall) Put(x, y); else { Put(x, y); Put(x, y + 1); } }
1917	    }
1918	    else if (SolidTileGen(x, y + 1) && y >= 0 && y < H && act[y * W + x] == 0 && y - 1 >= 0 && y - 1 < H && act[(y - 1) * W + x] == 0)
1919	    {
1920	        ushort bl = y + 1 < H ? typ[(y + 1) * W + x] : (ushort)0;
1921	        bool rockFam = bl == 1 || bl == 117 || bl == 25 || bl == 203;
1922	        if (rockFam) { if (preferSmall) Put(x, y); else { Put(x, y - 1); Put(x, y); } }
1923	        if (bl == 225) Put(x, y);
1924	        if (bl == 396 || bl == 397) { if (preferSmall) Put(x, y); else { Put(x, y - 1); Put(x, y); } }
1925	        if (bl == 368) { if (preferSmall) Put(x, y); else { Put(x, y - 1); Put(x, y); } }
1926	        if (bl == 367) { if (preferSmall) Put(x, y); else { Put(x, y - 1); Put(x, y); } }
1927	    }
1928	}
1929	void PlaceTightGen(int tx, int ty)   // cs:38329（生成期 shimmer 恒无 → 门禁只剩 231）
1930	{
1931	    if (tx < 0 || tx >= W || ty < 0 || ty >= H) return;
1932	    int tti = ty * W + tx;
1933	    if (act[tti] != 0 && typ[tti] == 231) return;
1934	    PlaceStalactite(tx, ty, (int)r.Next(2) == 0, (int)r.Next(3));
1935	}

1937	// ================= Pass 21 Marble（cs:12800 + MarbleBiome.cs）=================
1938	bool MarblePlace(int mox, int moy)
1939	{
1940	    if (BiomeTileCheck(mox, moy)) return false;
1941	    int bn = (int)r.Next(80, 150) / 3;
1942	    int bn2 = (int)r.Next(40, 60) / 3;
1943	    int bn3 = (bn2 * 3 - (int)r.Next(20, 30)) / 3;
1944	    int bOX = mox - bn * 3 / 2, bOY = moy - bn2 * 3 / 2;
1945	    // Slab 状态机（MarbleBiome.SlabStates）：0空 1实心 2半砖 3右下 4左下 5右上 6左上
1946	    var slabState = new int[bn + 2, bn2 + 2];
1947	    var slabWall = new bool[bn + 2, bn2 + 2];
1948	    bool GroupSolid(int gxx, int gyy)
1949	    {
1950	        int cnt = 0;
1951	        for (int a = 0; a < 3; a++) for (int b = 0; b < 3; b++) if (SolidTileGen(gxx + a, gyy + b)) cnt++;
1952	        return cnt > 0;   // scale/4*3 = 0（int 除）
1953	    }
1954	    for (int bi = -1; bi < bn + 1; bi++)
1955	    {
1956	        double bn4 = (double)(bi - bn / 2) / (double)bn + 0.5;
1957	        int bn5 = (int)((0.5 - Math.Abs(bn4 - 0.5)) * 5.0) - 2;
1958	        for (int bj = -1; bj < bn2 + 1; bj++)
1959	        {
1960	            bool bHasWall = true, bFlag = false;
1961	            bool bFlag2 = GroupSolid(bi * 3 + bOX, bj * 3 + bOY);
1962	            int bn6 = Math.Abs(bj - bn2 / 2) - bn3 / 4 + bn5;
1963	            if (bn6 > 3) { bFlag = bFlag2; bHasWall = false; }
1964	            else if (bn6 > 0) { bFlag = bj - bn2 / 2 > 0 || bFlag2; bHasWall = bj - bn2 / 2 < 0 || bn6 <= 2; }
1965	            else if (bn6 == 0) bFlag = (int)r.Next(2) == 0 && (bj - bn2 / 2 > 0 || bFlag2);
1966	            if (Math.Abs(bn4 - 0.5) > 0.35 + (double)r.NextDouble() * 0.1 && !bFlag2) { bHasWall = false; bFlag = false; }
1967	            slabState[bi + 1, bj + 1] = bFlag ? 1 : 0;
1968	            slabWall[bi + 1, bj + 1] = bHasWall;
1969	        }
1970	    }
1971	    bool SlabSolid(int st, int lx, int ly, int scale) => st switch
1972	    {
1973	        0 => false, 1 => true, 2 => ly >= scale / 2, 3 => lx >= scale - ly,
1974	        4 => lx < ly, 5 => lx > ly, 6 => lx < scale - ly, _ => true,
1975	    };
1976	    for (int bk = 0; bk < bn; bk++)          // SmoothSlope（slab 状态形状重分类）
1977	        for (int bl = 0; bl < bn2; bl++)
1978	        {
1979	            int sx2 = bk + 1, sy2 = bl + 1;
1980	            if (slabState[sx2, sy2] == 0) continue;
1981	            bool up = slabState[sx2, sy2 - 1] != 0, dn = slabState[sx2, sy2 + 1] != 0;
1982	            bool lf = slabState[sx2 - 1, sy2] != 0, rt = slabState[sx2 + 1, sy2] != 0;
1983	            int code = ((up ? 1 : 0) << 3) | ((dn ? 1 : 0) << 2) | ((lf ? 1 : 0) << 1) | (rt ? 1 : 0);
1984	            slabState[sx2, sy2] = code switch { 10 => 6, 9 => 5, 6 => 4, 5 => 3, 4 => 2, _ => 1 };
1985	        }
1986	    int bn7 = bn / 2, bn8 = bn2 / 2;
1987	    int bn9 = (bn8 + 1) * (bn8 + 1);
1988	    double bvv = (double)r.NextDouble() * 2.0 - 1.0;
1989	    double bvv2 = (double)r.NextDouble() * 2.0 - 1.0;
1990	    double bvv3 = (double)r.NextDouble() * 2.0 - 1.0;
1991	    double bn11 = 0.0;
1992	    for (int bm = 0; bm <= bn; bm++)
1993	    {
1994	        double bn12 = (double)bn8 / (double)bn7 * (double)(bm - bn7);
1995	        int bn13 = Math.Min(bn8, (int)Math.Sqrt(Math.Max(0.0, (double)bn9 - bn12 * bn12)));
1996	        bn11 = bm >= bn / 2 ? bn11 + (bvv2 + (bvv3 - bvv2) * ((double)bm / (double)(bn / 2) - 1.0))
1997	                            : bn11 + (bvv + (bvv2 - bvv) * ((double)bm / (double)(bn / 2)));
1998	        for (int bnn = bn8 - bn13; bnn <= bn8 + bn13; bnn++)
1999	        {
2000	            int pOX = bm * 3 + bOX, pOY = bnn * 3 + bOY + (int)bn11;
2001	            int pst = slabState[bm + 1, bnn + 1];
2002	            bool pWall = slabWall[bm + 1, bnn + 1];
2003	            int pn4 = 0, pn5 = 3;             // PlaceSlab(scale=3)
2004	            for (int pi = -1; pi < 4; pi++)
2005	            {
2006	                if ((pi == -1 || pi == 3) && (int)r.Next(2) == 0) continue;
2007	                if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0) pn4--;
2008	                if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0) pn5++;
2009	                for (int pj = pn4; pj < pn5; pj++)
2010	                {
2011	                    int px = pOX + pi, py = pOY + pj;
2012	                    if (px < 0 || px >= W || py < 0 || py >= H) continue;
2013	                    int pti = py * W + px;
2014	                    typ[pti] = IsOre(typ[pti]) ? typ[pti] : (ushort)367;   // ResetToType：矿石保留
2015	                    act[pti] = SlabSolid(pst, pi, pj, 3) ? (byte)1 : (byte)0;
2016	                    liq[pti] = 0;
2017	                    if (pWall) wal[pti] = 178;
2018	                    if (SolidTileGen(px, py - 1) && (int)r.Next(4) == 0) PlaceTightGen(px, py);
2019	                    if (SolidTileGen(px, py) && (int)r.Next(4) == 0) PlaceTightGen(px, py - 1);
2020	                }
2021	            }
2022	        }
2023	    }
2024	    return true;
2025	}
2026	{
2027	    int mbCount = (int)r.Next(4, 9);                 // WorldGenRange(4,8,WorldArea) scale=1
2028	    int mbPlaced = 0, mbFails = 0;
2029	    while (mbPlaced < mbCount)
2030	    {
2031	        double mb5 = (double)mbPlaced / (double)mbCount;
2032	        int mbX = 0, mbY = 0; bool mbFlag = false;
2033	        int mb6 = 100;
2034	        while (!mbFlag)
2035	        {
2036	            mb6--;
2037	            if (mb6 <= 0) break;
2038	            int mbx0 = (int)(mb5 * (double)(W - 200)) + 100;
2039	            mbX = (int)r.Next(mbx0, mbx0 + (int)((double)(W - 160) / (double)mbCount));
2040	            int mby0 = (int)gvRockLayer + 20;        // GenVars.rockLayer（raw double 截断）
2041	            mbY = (int)r.Next(mby0, mby0 + (H - ((int)gvRockLayer + 40) - 200));
2042	            while ((double)mbX > W * 0.45 && (double)mbX < W * 0.55) mbX = (int)r.Next(BEACH_DISTANCE, W - BEACH_DISTANCE);
2043	            mbFlag = true;
2044	        }
2045	        mbFails++;
2046	        if (mbFlag && MarblePlace(mbX, mbY)) { mbPlaced++; mbFails = 0; }
2047	        else if (mbFails > W * 10) { mbCount = mbPlaced; mbPlaced++; mbFails = 0; }
2048	    }
2049	}
2050	Checkpoint("marble");

2052	// ================= Pass 22 Granite（cs:12884 + GraniteBiome.cs）=================
2053	void GranitePlace(int gox0, int goy0)
2054	{
2055	    if (gox0 < 0 || gox0 >= W || goy0 < 0 || goy0 >= H || act[goy0 * W + gox0] != 0) return;
2056	    int gox = gox0 - 100, goy = goy0 - 100;          // 200×200 magma 图
2057	    var gPres = new double[200, 200];
2058	    var gRes = new double[200, 200];
2059	    var gAct = new bool[200, 200];
2060	    for (int a = 0; a < 200; a++)
2061	        for (int b = 0; b < 200; b++)
2062	            gRes[a, b] = SolidTileGen(a + gox, b + goy) ? 4.0 : 1.0;
2063	    // 8 邻域单位向量（Vector2D.Normalize）
2064	    double[] nvx = { -0.7071067811865476, -1, -0.7071067811865476, 0, 0, 0, 0.7071067811865476, 1, 0.7071067811865476 };
2065	    double[] nvy = { -0.7071067811865476, 0, 0.7071067811865476, -1, 0, 1, -0.7071067811865476, 0, 0.7071067811865476 };
2066	    int gx3 = 100, gx4 = 100, gy3 = 100, gy4 = 100;
2067	    var tPres = new double[200, 200];
2068	    var tRes = new double[200, 200];
2069	    var tAct = new bool[200, 200];
2070	    for (int it = 0; it < 300; it++)
2071	    {
2072	        for (int a = 0; a < 200; a++) for (int b = 0; b < 200; b++) { tPres[a, b] = gPres[a, b]; tRes[a, b] = gRes[a, b]; tAct[a, b] = gAct[a, b]; }
2073	        for (int j = gx3; j <= gx4; j++)
2074	            for (int k = gy3; k <= gy4; k++)
2075	            {
2076	                if (!gAct[j, k]) continue;
2077	                double sum = 0.0, vx = 0.0, vy = 0.0;
2078	                for (int l = -1; l <= 1; l++)
2079	                    for (int m = -1; m <= 1; m++)
2080	                    {
2081	                        if (l == 0 && m == 0) continue;
2082	                        int vi = (l + 1) * 3 + (m + 1);
2083	                        double np = gPres[j + l, k + m];
2084	                        if (gPres[j, k] > 0.01 && !gAct[j + l, k + m])
2085	                        {
2086	                            if (l == -1) gx3 = Math.Clamp(j + l, 1, gx3); else gx4 = Math.Clamp(j + l, gx4, 198);
2087	                            if (m == -1) gy3 = Math.Clamp(k + m, 1, gy3); else gy4 = Math.Clamp(k + m, gy4, 198);
2088	                            tAct[j + l, k + m] = true;
2089	                        }
2090	                        sum += np;
2091	                        vx += np * nvx[vi]; vy += np * nvy[vi];
2092	                    }
2093	                sum /= 8.0;
2094	                if (sum > gRes[j, k])
2095	                {
2096	                    double len = Math.Sqrt(vx * vx + vy * vy) / 8.0;
2097	                    double nv = Math.Max(sum - len - gPres[j, k], 0.0) + len + gPres[j, k] * 0.875 - gRes[j, k];
2098	                    nv = Math.Max(0.0, nv);
2099	                    tPres[j, k] = nv;
2100	                    tRes[j, k] = Math.Max(0.0, gRes[j, k] - nv * 0.02);
2101	                }
2102	            }
2103	        if (it < 2) { tPres[100, 100] = 25.0; tAct[100, 100] = true; }
2104	        var sp1 = gPres; gPres = tPres; tPres = sp1;
2105	        var sp2 = gRes; gRes = tRes; tRes = sp2;
2106	        var sp3 = gAct; gAct = tAct; tAct = sp3;
2107	    }
2108	    bool GraniteUseLava()
2109	    {
2110	        if (goy + 100 <= lavaLine - 30) return false;
2111	        for (int a = -50; a < 50; a++)
2112	            for (int b = -50; b < 50; b++)
2113	            {
2114	                int ax = gox + 100 + a, ay = goy + 100 + b;
2115	                if (ax >= 10 && ax < W - 10 && ay >= 10 && ay < H - 10 && act[ay * W + ax] != 0)
2116	                {
2117	                    ushort t = typ[ay * W + ax];
2118	                    if (t == 147 || t == 200 || (t >= 161 && t <= 163)) return false;
2119	                }
2120	            }
2121	        return true;
2122	    }
2123	    bool useLava = GraniteUseLava();
2124	    for (int i = gx3; i <= gx4; i++)
2125	        for (int j = gy3; j <= gy4; j++)
2126	        {
2127	            int tx = gox + i, ty = goy + j;
2128	            if (tx < 10 || tx >= W - 10 || ty < 10 || ty >= H - 10 || !gAct[i, j]) continue;
2129	            int ti = ty * W + tx;
2130	            double gnum = Math.Sin((double)(goy + j) * 0.4) * 0.7 + 1.2;
2131	            double gnum2 = 0.2 + 0.5 / Math.Sqrt(Math.Max(0.0, gPres[i, j] - gRes[i, j]));
2132	            if (Math.Max(1.0 - Math.Max(0.0, gnum * gnum2), gPres[i, j] / 15.0) > 0.35 + (SolidTileGen(tx, ty) ? 0.0 : 0.5))
2133	            {
2134	                typ[ti] = IsOre(typ[ti]) ? typ[ti] : (ushort)368;
2135	                act[ti] = 1; liq[ti] = 0;
2136	                wal[ti] = 180;
2137	            }
2138	            else if (gRes[i, j] < 0.01)
2139	            {
2140	                act[ti] = 0;                       // ClearTile
2141	                wal[ti] = 180;
2142	            }
2143	            if (liq[ti] > 0 && useLava) liqT[ti] = 1;
2144	        }
2145	    // CleanupTiles：3×3 实心邻 <3 的活性花岗岩清除
2146	    var cleanList = new List<(int x, int y)>();
2147	    for (int i = gx3; i <= gx4; i++)
2148	        for (int j = gy3; j <= gy4; j++)
2149	        {
2150	            if (!gAct[i, j]) continue;
2151	            int tx = gox + i, ty = goy + j;
2152	            if (tx < 10 || tx >= W - 10 || ty < 10 || ty >= H - 10 || !SolidTileGen(tx, ty)) continue;
2153	            int cnt = 0;
2154	            for (int a = -1; a <= 1; a++) for (int b = -1; b <= 1; b++) if (SolidTileGen(tx + a, ty + b)) cnt++;
2155	            if (cnt < 3) cleanList.Add((tx, ty));
2156	        }
2157	    foreach (var cp in cleanList) { act[cp.y * W + cp.x] = 0; wal[cp.y * W + cp.x] = 180; }
2158	    // PlaceDecorations：FastRandom(seed).WithModifier(65440) 独立流
2159	    var gFr = new FR((ulong)(long)seed).Mod(65440UL);
2160	    for (int i = gx3; i <= gx4; i++)
2161	        for (int j = gy3; j <= gy4; j++)
2162	        {
2163	            int tx = gox + i, ty = goy + j;
2164	            if (tx < 10 || tx >= W - 10 || ty < 10 || ty >= H - 10 || !gAct[i, j]) continue;
2165	            var gFr2 = gFr.ModXY(tx, ty);
2166	            if (gFr2.Next(8) == 0 && act[ty * W + tx] != 0)
2167	            {
2168	                if (ty + 1 < H && act[(ty + 1) * W + tx] == 0) PlaceStalactite(tx, ty + 1, gFr2.Next(2) == 0, gFr2.Next(3));
2169	                if (ty - 1 >= 0 && act[(ty - 1) * W + tx] == 0) PlaceStalactite(tx, ty - 1, gFr2.Next(2) == 0, gFr2.Next(3));
2170	            }
2171	            if (gFr2.Next(2) == 0) { /* Tile.SmoothSlope —— 坡面哈希无关 */ }
2172	        }
2173	}
2174	{
2175	    int gnCount = (int)r.Next(4, 9);                 // WorldGenRange(4,8,WorldWidth) scale=1
2176	    var gnPts = new List<(int x, int y)>();
2177	    int gnPlaced = 0, gnFails = 0;
2178	    while (gnPlaced < gnCount)
2179	    {
2180	        double gn5 = (double)gnPlaced / (double)gnCount;
2181	        int gnX = 0, gnY = 0; bool gnFlag = false;
2182	        int gn7 = 100;
2183	        while (!gnFlag)
2184	        {
2185	            gn7--;
2186	            if (gn7 <= 0) break;
2187	            int gnx0 = (int)(gn5 * (double)(W - 200)) + 100;
2188	            gnX = (int)r.Next(gnx0, gnx0 + (int)((double)(W - 200) / (double)gnCount));
2189	            int gny0 = (int)gvRockLayer + 20;
2190	            gnY = (int)r.Next(gny0, gny0 + (H - ((int)gvRockLayer + 40) - 200));
2191	            while ((double)gnX > W * 0.45 && (double)gnX < W * 0.55) gnX = (int)r.Next(BEACH_DISTANCE, W - BEACH_DISTANCE);
2192	            gnFlag = true;
2193	        }
2194	        gnFails++;
2195	        if (gnFlag && !BiomeTileCheck(gnX, gnY) && !(gnX >= 0 && gnX < W && gnY >= 0 && gnY < H && act[gnY * W + gnX] != 0))
2196	        { gnPts.Add((gnX, gnY)); gnPlaced++; }
2197	        else if (gnFails > W * 10) { gnCount = gnPlaced; gnPlaced++; gnFails = 0; }
2198	    }
2199	    for (int gi = 0; gi < gnCount && gi < gnPts.Count; gi++) GranitePlace(gnPts[gi].x, gnPts[gi].y);
2200	}

2204	bool WaterStaysPut(int wx, int wy)   // WillWaterPlacedHereStayPut（cs:79050）
2205	{
2206	    bool Side(int ax, int ay)
2207	    {
2208	        if (ax < 0 || ax >= W || ay < 0 || ay >= H) return false;
2209	        int ti = ay * W + ax;
2210	        return (act[ti] != 0 && SOLID.Contains(typ[ti])) || liq[ti] == 255;
2211	    }
2212	    return Side(wx, wy + 1) && Side(wx - 1, wy) && Side(wx + 1, wy);
2213	}

2667	// GrowAshTree —— JS TreePass.growTreeWithSettings(ASH_PROFILE) 哈希通道镜像。
2668	// 只需 type/active 落点一致 + 掷骰序一致（帧值不入哈希）：干身行循环的
2669	// num6(含冲突重掷)/num5/左右枝 k、tuft 双门、基座、树冠骰全部照 JS 顺序。
2670	void GrowAshTree(int tx, int ty)
2671	{
2672	    // 越过树苗（灰烬区无树苗 → 立即停）
2673	    int num = ty;
2674	    while (num < H && act[num * W + tx] != 0 && typ[num * W + tx] == 590) num++;   // 树苗 590
2675	    if (num >= H || num < 1) return;
2676	    // 基座上一格三列无液体
2677	    if (liq[(num - 1) * W + tx - 1] != 0 || liq[(num - 1) * W + tx] != 0 || liq[(num - 1) * W + tx + 1] != 0) return;
2678	    int gi = num * W + tx;
2679	    if (act[gi] == 0) return;                                  // 基座须整砖（half/slope 此阶段无）
2680	    if (!TREE_WALLS.Contains(wal[(num - 1) * W + tx])) return; // 墙白名单
2681	    if (typ[gi] != 633) return;                                // AshTreeGroundTest：仅灰烬草
2682	    bool sideFit(int dx) => tx + dx >= 0 && tx + dx < W && act[num * W + tx + dx] != 0 && typ[num * W + tx + dx] == 633;
2683	    if (!sideFit(-1) && !sideFit(1)) return;
2684	    int height = (int)r.Next(7, 13);
2685	    int num4 = height + 4;                                     // TreeTopPaddingNeeded = 4
2686	    if (tx - 2 < 0 || tx + 2 >= W || num - num4 < 0) return;
2687	    for (int ex = tx - 2; ex <= tx + 2; ex++)
2688	        for (int ey = num - num4; ey <= num - 1; ey++)
2689	        {
2690	            int ei = ey * W + ex;
2691	            if (act[ei] == 0) continue;
2692	            if (!EMPTY_ALLOW.Contains(typ[ei])) return;        // EmptyTileCheck 白名单
2693	        }
2694	    void Put(int ax, int ay) { if (ax >= 0 && ax < W && ay >= 0 && ay < H) { typ[ay * W + ax] = 634; act[ay * W + ax] = 1; } }
2695	    bool flagL = false, flagR = false;
2696	    for (int i = num - height; i < num; i++)
2697	    {
2698	        int num6 = (int)r.Next(10);
2699	        if (i == num - 1 || i == num - height) num6 = 0;
2700	        while (((num6 == 5 || num6 == 7) && flagL) || ((num6 == 6 || num6 == 7) && flagR)) num6 = (int)r.Next(10);
2701	        flagL = false; flagR = false;
2702	        if (num6 == 5 || num6 == 7) flagL = true;
2703	        if (num6 == 6 || num6 == 7) flagR = true;
2704	        int num5 = (int)r.Next(3);                              // 干身帧骰（帧值不入哈希）
2705	        Put(tx, i);
2706	        if (num6 == 5 || num6 == 7) { int k = (int)r.Next(3); int f = (int)r.Next(3); Put(tx - 1, i); }   // 左枝 2 骰
2707	        if (num6 == 6 || num6 == 7) { int k = (int)r.Next(3); int f = (int)r.Next(3); Put(tx + 1, i); }   // 右枝 2 骰
2708	    }
2709	    // 基座 tuft：两侧地面须灰烬草（GEM_TREE_FIT 口径此处即 633）——地狱腔天然全跳
2710	    bool tuftFit(int dx) => tx + dx >= 0 && tx + dx < W && act[num * W + tx + dx] != 0 && typ[num * W + tx + dx] == 633;
2711	    bool tL = tuftFit(-1), tR = tuftFit(1);
2712	    if ((int)r.Next(3) == 0) tL = false;
2713	    if ((int)r.Next(3) == 0) tR = false;
2714	    if (tR) { int k = (int)r.Next(3); Put(tx + 1, num - 1); }
2715	    if (tL) { int k = (int)r.Next(3); Put(tx - 1, num - 1); }
2716	    if (tL || tR) { int k = (int)r.Next(3); Put(tx, num - 1); }
2717	    int crownFx = (int)r.Next(13) != 0 ? 22 : 0;               // 帧值不入哈希，仅保骰
2718	    int crownK = (int)r.Next(3);
2719	    Put(tx, num - height);
2720	}

2767	            double uwS = (int)r.Next(1, 3);
2768	            if ((int)r.Next(3) == 0) uwS *= 0.5;
2769	            if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0)

2771	            if ((int)r.Next(2) == 0)
2772	            {
2773	                uwS = (int)r.Next(1, 3);
2774	                TileRunner(ux, uy - (int)r.Next(2, 5), (int)((double)(int)r.Next(5, 15) * uwS), (int)((double)(int)r.Next(10, 15) * uwS), 57, true, -1.0, 0.3);
2775	            }
2776	            TileRunner(ux + (int)r.Next(-10, 10), uy + (int)r.Next(-10, 10), (int)r.Next(5, 15), (int)r.Next(5, 10), -2, false, (int)r.Next(-1, 3), (int)r.Next(-1, 3));
2777	            if ((int)r.Next(3) == 0)
2778	                TileRunner(ux + (int)r.Next(-10, 10), uy + (int)r.Next(-10, 10), (int)r.Next(10, 30), (int)r.Next(10, 20), -2, false, (int)r.Next(-1, 3), (int)r.Next(-1, 3));

2838	bool CanEvilReplace(int cx, int cy)
2839	{
2840	    int t = act[cy * W + cx] != 0 ? typ[cy * W + cx] : -1;
2841	    ushort wl = wal[cy * W + cx];
2842	    if (t >= 0 && (TILE_DUNGEON.Contains((ushort)t) || (t >= 481 && t <= 483))) return false;
2843	    if (WALL_DUNGEON.Contains(wl)) return false;
2844	    return true;
2845	}
2846	bool IsTileNearby26(int nx, int ny, int type, int distance)
2847	{
2848	    for (int i = nx - distance; i <= nx + distance; i++)
2849	        for (int j = ny - distance; j <= ny + distance; j++)
2850	        {
2851	            if (i < 0 || i >= W || j < 0 || j >= H) continue;
2852	            int ti = j * W + i;
2853	            if (act[ti] != 0 && typ[ti] == (ushort)type) return true;
2854	        }
2855	    return false;
2856	}

2867	    }

2870	    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
2871	        for (int j = 0; j < 2; j++)
2872	        {
